event: file-complete
data: {"agent":"specialist-toa","file":"src/utils/loanCalculator.ts","content":"import { LoanParams, YearlySchedule, LoanCalculationResult } from '../types';\n\n/**\n * 住宅ローンの毎月返済額、返済総額、利息総額、年次返済スケジュールを計算する\n */\nexport function calculateLoan(params: LoanParams): LoanCalculationResult {\n  const { principal, interestRate, years, method, bonusAmount = 0 } = params;\n\n  if (principal <= 0 || years <= 0) {\n    return {\n      monthlyPayment: 0,\n      monthlyPaymentMax: 0,\n      totalPayment: 0,\n      totalInterest: 0,\n      yearlySchedule: [],\n    };\n  }\n\n  const totalMonths = years * 12;\n  const monthlyRate = interestRate > 0 ? interestRate / 100 / 12 : 0;\n\n  // ボーナス返済分の元金按分計算（ボーナス返済がある場合）\n  // bonusAmount は年2回（1回あたり）の加算返済額\n  // ボーナス返済額から逆算してボーナス返済用元金を算出\n  const bonusCount = years * 2;\n  const semiAnnualRate = interestRate > 0 ? interestRate / 100 / 2 : 0;\n\n  let bonusPrincipal = 0;\n  if (bonusAmount > 0 && bonusCount > 0) {\n    if (semiAnnualRate > 0) {\n      // 元利均等でのボーナス現在価値相当\n      const factor = Math.pow(1 + semiAnnualRate, bonusCount);\n      bonusPrincipal = Math.min(\n        principal * 0.5, // 借入額の最大50%をボーナス分上限とする安全策\n        Math.round((bonusAmount * (factor - 1)) / (semiAnnualRate * factor))\n      );\n    } else {\n      bonusPrincipal = Math.min(principal * 0.5, bonusAmount * bonusCount);\n    }\n  }\n\n  const monthlyLoanPrincipal = principal - bonusPrincipal;\n\n  let firstMonthPayment = 0;\n  let lastMonthPayment = 0;\n  let totalPaymentSum = 0;\n  let totalInterestSum = 0;\n\n  let currentBalance = principal;\n  const schedule: YearlySchedule[] = [];\n\n  if (method === 'equal-payment') {\n    // 【元利均等返済】\n    // 毎月の均等返済額（毎月分）\n    let standardMonthlyPayment = 0;\n    if (monthlyRate > 0) {\n      const factor = Math.pow(1 + monthlyRate, totalMonths);\n      standardMonthlyPayment = (monthlyLoanPrincipal * monthlyRate * factor) / (factor - 1);\n    } else {\n      standardMonthlyPayment = monthlyLoanPrincipal / totalMonths;\n    }\n\n    firstMonthPayment = Math.round(standardMonthlyPayment);\n    lastMonthPayment = Math.round(standardMonthlyPayment);\n\n    let cumulativeTotalPaid = 0;\n\n    for (let y = 1; y <= years; y++) {\n      let yearPrincipal = 0;\n      let yearInterest = 0;\n\n      for (let m = 1; m <= 12; m++) {\n        if (currentBalance <= 0) break;\n\n        const interest = currentBalance * monthlyRate;\n        let pmt = standardMonthlyPayment;\n\n        // ボーナス月（6月・12月）加算\n        if ((m === 6 || m === 12) && bonusAmount > 0) {\n          pmt += bonusAmount;\n        }\n\n        let principalPaid = pmt - interest;\n\n        // 最終月の残債超過調整\n        if (principalPaid > currentBalance || (y === years && m === 12)) {\n          principalPaid = currentBalance;\n          pmt = principalPaid + interest;\n        }\n\n        currentBalance -= principalPaid;\n        yearPrincipal += principalPaid;\n        yearInterest += interest;\n        cumulativeTotalPaid += pmt;\n      }\n\n      totalPaymentSum += yearPrincipal + yearInterest;\n      totalInterestSum += yearInterest;\n\n      schedule.push({\n        year: y,\n        remainingBalance: Math.max(0, Math.round(currentBalance)),\n        yearlyPrincipal: Math.round(yearPrincipal),\n        yearlyInterest: Math.round(yearInterest),\n        totalPaid: Math.round(cumulativeTotalPaid),\n      });\n    }\n  } else {\n    // 【元金均等返済】\n    const monthlyPrincipalFixed = monthlyLoanPrincipal / totalMonths;\n    const bonusPrincipalFixed = bonusCount > 0 ? bonusPrincipal / bonusCount : 0;\n\n    firstMonthPayment = Math.round(monthlyPrincipalFixed + principal * monthlyRate);\n    lastMonthPayment = Math.round(monthlyPrincipalFixed + monthlyPrincipalFixed * monthlyRate);\n\n    let cumulativeTotalPaid = 0;\n\n    for (let y = 1; y <= years; y++) {\n      let yearPrincipal = 0;\n      let yearInterest = 0;\n\n      for (let m = 1; m <= 12; m++) {\n        if (currentBalance <= 0) break;\n\n        const interest = currentBalance * monthlyRate;\n        let principalPaid = monthlyPrincipalFixed;\n\n        // ボーナス月（6月・12月）の元金加算\n        if ((m === 6 || m === 12) && bonusPrincipalFixed > 0) {\n          principalPaid += bonusPrincipalFixed;\n        }\n\n        // 最終月調整\n        if (principalPaid > currentBalance || (y === years && m === 12)) {\n          principalPaid = currentBalance;\n        }\n\n        const pmt = principalPaid + interest;\n        currentBalance -= principalPaid;\n        yearPrincipal += principalPaid;\n        yearInterest += interest;\n        cumulativeTotalPaid += pmt;\n      }\n\n      totalPaymentSum += yearPrincipal + yearInterest;\n      totalInterestSum += yearInterest;\n\n      schedule.push({\n        year: y,\n        remainingBalance: Math.max(0, Math.round(currentBalance)),\n        yearlyPrincipal: Math.round(yearPrincipal),\n        yearlyInterest: Math.round(yearInterest),\n        totalPaid: Math.round(cumulativeTotalPaid),\n      });\n    }\n  }\n\n  return {\n    monthlyPayment: method === 'equal-principal' ? firstMonthPayment : firstMonthPayment,\n    monthlyPaymentMax: method === 'equal-principal' ? firstMonthPayment : undefined,\n    totalPayment: Math.round(totalPaymentSum),\n    totalInterest: Math.round(totalInterestSum),\n    yearlySchedule: schedule,\n  };\n}","language":"typescript"}

100 : val));\n    setPrincipalManYen(clamped);\n  };\n\n  // 金利入力ハンドラ\n  const handleRateChange = (val: number) => {\n    const clamped = Math.max(0.01, Math.min(15.0, isNaN(val) ? 0.01 : val));\n    setInterestRate(Math.round(clamped * 100) / 100);\n  };\n\n  // 返済年数変更ハンドラ\n  const handleYearsChange = (val: number) => {\n    const clamped = Math.max(1, Math.min(50, isNaN(val) ?            <div className=\"input-unit\">\n                <input\n                  id=\"principal-input\"\n                  type=\"number\"\n                  className=\"number-input\"\n event: file-complete
data: {"agent":"specialist-toa","file":"src/utils/loanCalculator.ts","content":"import { LoanParams, YearlySchedule, LoanCalculationResult } from '../types';\n\n/**\n * 住宅ローンの毎月返済額、返済総額、利息総額、年次返済スケジュールを計算する\n */\nexport function calculateLoan(params: LoanParams): LoanCalculationResult {\n  const { principal, interestRate, years, method, bonusAmount = 0 } = params;\n\n  if (principal <= 0 || years <= 0) {\n    return {\n      monthlyPayment: 0,\n      monthlyPaymentMax: 0,\n      monthlyPaymentMin: 0,\n      totalPayment: 0,\n      totalInterest: 0,\n      yearlySchedule: [],\n    };\n  }\n\n  const totalMonths = years * 12;\n  const monthlyRate = interestRate > 0 ? interestRate / 100 / 12 : 0;\n\n  // ボーナス返済分の元金按分計算（ボーナス返済がある場合）\n  // bonusAmount は年2回（1回あたり）の加算返済額\n  const bonusCount = years * 2;\n  const semiAnnualRate = interestRate > 0 ? interestRate / 100 / 2 : 0;\n\n  let bonusPrincipal = 0;\n  if (bonusAmount > 0 && bonusCount > 0) {\n    if (semiAnnualRate > 0.000001) {\n      // 元利均等でのボーナス現在価値相当\n      const factor = Math.pow(1 + semiAnnualRate, bonusCount);\n      const denominator = semiAnnualRate * factor;\n      if (denominator > 0 && factor > 1) {\n        const calculatedBonusPrincipal = Math.round((bonusAmount * (factor - 1)) / denominator);\n        bonusPrincipal = Math.min(principal * 0.5, Math.max(0, calculatedBonusPrincipal));\n      } else {\n        bonusPrincipal = Math.min(principal * 0.5, Math.max(0, bonusAmount * bonusCount));\n      }\n    } else {\n      bonusPrincipal = Math.min(principal * 0.5, Math.max(0, bonusAmount * bonusCount));\n    }\n  }\n\n  const monthlyLoanPrincipal = Math.max(0, principal - bonusPrincipal);\n\n  let firstMonthPayment = 0;\n  let lastMonthPayment = 0;\n  let totalPaymentSum = 0;\n  let totalInterestSum = 0;\n\n  let currentBalance = principal;\n  const schedule: YearlySchedule[] = [];\n\n  if (method === 'equal-payment') {\n    // 【元利均等返済】\n    let standardMonthlyPayment = 0;\n    if (monthlyRate > 0) {\n      const factor = Math.pow(1 + monthlyRate, totalMonths);\n      standardMonthlyPayment =\n        factor > 1 ? (monthlyLoanPrincipal * monthlyRate * factor) / (factor - 1) : 0;\n    } else {\n      standardMonthlyPayment = totalMonths > 0 ? monthlyLoanPrincipal / totalMonths : 0;\n    }\n\n    firstMonthPayment = Math.round(standardMonthlyPayment);\n    lastMonthPayment = Math.round(standardMonthlyPayment);\n\n    let cumulativeTotalPaid = 0;\n\n    for (let y = 1; y <= years; y++) {\n      let yearPrincipal = 0;\n      let yearInterest = 0;\n\n      for (let m = 1; m <= 12; m++) {\n        if (currentBalance <= 0) break;\n\n        const interest = currentBalance * monthlyRate;\n        let pmt = standardMonthlyPayment;\n\n        // ボーナス月（6月・12月）加算\n        if ((m === 6 || m === 12) && bonusAmount > 0) {\n          pmt += bonusAmount;\n        }\n\n        let principalPaid = pmt - interest;\n\n        // 最終月の残債超過調整\n        if (principalPaid > currentBalance || (y === years && m === 12)) {\n          principalPaid = currentBalance;\n          pmt = principalPaid + interest;\n        }\n\n        currentBalance -= principalPaid;\n        yearPrincipal += principalPaid;\n        yearInterest += interest;\n        cumulativeTotalPaid += pmt;\n      }\n\n      totalPaymentSum += yearPrincipal + yearInterest;\n      totalInterestSum += yearInterest;\n\n      schedule.push({\n        year: y,\n        remainingBalance: Math.max(0, Math.round(currentBalance)),\n        yearlyPrincipal: Math.round(yearPrincipal),\n        yearlyInterest: Math.round(yearInterest),\n        totalPaid: Math.round(cumulativeTotalPaid),\n      });\n    }\n  } else {\n    // 【元金均等返済】\n    const monthlyPrincipalFixed = totalMonths > 0 ? monthlyLoanPrincipal / totalMonths : 0;\n    const bonusPrincipalFixed = bonusCount > 0 ? bonusPrincipal / bonusCount : 0;\n\n    firstMonthPayment = Math.round(monthlyPrincipalFixed + principal * monthlyRate);\n    lastMonthPayment = Math.max(\n      0,\n      Math.round(monthlyPrincipalFixed + monthlyPrincipalFixed * monthlyRate)\n    );\n\n    let cumulativeTotalPaid = 0;\n\n    for (let y = 1; y <= years; y++) {\n      let yearPrincipal = 0;\n      let yearInterest = 0;\n\n      for (let m = 1; m <= 12; m++) {\n        if (currentBalance <= 0) break;\n\n        const interest = currentBalance * monthlyRate;\n        let principalPaid = monthlyPrincipalFixed;\n\n        // ボーナス月（6月・12月）の元金加算\n        if ((m === 6 || m === 12) && bonusPrincipalFixed > 0) {\n          principalPaid += bonusPrincipalFixed;\n        }\n\n        // 最終月調整\n        if (principalPaid > currentBalance || (y === years && m === 12)) {\n          principalPaid = currentBalance;\n        }\n\n        const pmt = principalPaid + interest;\n        currentBalance -= principalPaid;\n        yearPrincipal += principalPaid;\n        yearInterest += interest;\n        cumulativeTotalPaid += pmt;\n      }\n\n      totalPaymentSum += yearPrincipal + yearInterest;\n      totalInterestSum += yearInterest;\n\n      schedule.push({\n        year: y,\n        remainingBalance: Math.max(0, Math.round(currentBalance)),\n        yearlyPrincipal: Math.round(yearPrincipal),\n        yearlyInterest: Math.round(yearInterest),\n        totalPaid: Math.round(cumulativeTotalPaid),\n      });\n    }\n  }\n\n  return {\n    monthlyPayment: firstMonthPayment,\n    monthlyPaymentMax: method === 'equal-principal' ? firstMonthPayment : undefined,\n    monthlyPaymentMin: method === 'equal-principal' ? lastMonthPayment : undefined,\n    totalPayment: Math.round(totalPaymentSum),\n    totalInterest: Math.round(totalInterestSum),\n    yearlySchedule: schedule,\n  };\n}","language":"typescript"}

LIMITS.PRINCIPAL.MIN : val)\n    );\n    setPrincipalManYen(clamped);\n  };\n\n  // 金利入力ハンドラ\n  const handleRateChange = (val: number) => {\n    const clamped = Math.max(\n      LIMITS.RATE.MIN,\n      Math.min(LIMITS.RATE.MAX, isNaN(val) ? LIMITS.RATE.MIN : val)\n    );\n    setInterestRate(Math.round(clamped * 100) / 100);\n  };\n\n  // 返済年数変更ハンドラ\n  const handleYearsChange = (val: number) => {\n    const clamped = Math.max(\n      LIMITS.YEARS.MIN,\n      Math.min(LIMITS.YEARS.MAX, isNaN(val) ? LIMITS.YEARS.MIN : val)\n    );\n    setYears(clamped);\n  };\n\n  // ボーナス返済額変更ハンドラ\n  const handleBonusChange = (val: number) => {\n    const clamped = Math.max(\n      LIMITS.BONUS.MIN,\n      Math.min(LIMITS.BONUS.MAX, isNaN(val) ? LIMITS.BONUS.MIN : val)\n    );\n    setBonusManYen(clamped);\n  };\n\n  return (\n    <div className=\"app-container\">\n      <header className=\"app-header\">\n        <span className=\"header-badge\">Mortgage Calculator</span>\n        <h1>住宅ローン返済シミュレーター</h1>\n        <p>\n          借入額・金利・返済年数から毎月の返済額・返済総額・利息内訳を即座に計算し、年別推移をグラフと表で確認できます。\n        </p>\n      </header>\n\n      <div className=\"calculator-grid\">\n        {/* 入力フォームカード */}\n        <section className=\"card\">\n          <h2 className=\"card-title\">ローン借入条件の設定</h2>\n\n          {/* 借入金額 */}\n          <div className=\"form-group\">\n            <div className=\"label-row\">\n              <label htmlFor=\"principal-input\">借入希望額</label>\n              <span style={{ fontSize: '12px', color: 'var(--color-text-secondary)' }}>\n                {principalManYen >= 10000\n                  ? `${Math.floor(principalManYen / 10000)}億${\n                      principalManYen % 10000 > 0 ? (principalManYen % 10000) + '万' : ''\n                    }円`\n                  : `${principalManYen.toLocaleString()}万円`}\n              </span>\n            </div>\n            <div className=\"slider-input-group\">\n              <div className=\"input-unit\">\n                <input\n                  id=\"principal-input\"\n                  type=\"number\"\n                  className=\"number-input\"\n                  min={LIMITS.PRINCIPAL.MIN}\n                  max={LIMITS.PRINCIPAL.MAX}\n                  step={LIMITS.PRINCIPAL.STEP_NUMBER}\n                  value={principalManYen}\n                  onChange={(e) => handlePrincipalChange(Number(e.target.value))}\n                />\n                <span>万円</span>\n              </div>\n              <input\n                type=\"range\"\n                className=\"slider\"\n                min={LIMITS.PRINCIPAL.MIN}\n                max={LIMITS.PRINCIPAL.MAX}\n                step={LIMITS.PRINCIPAL.STEP_SLIDER}\n                value={principalManYen}\n                onChange={(e) => handlePrincipalChange(Number(e.target.value))}\n              />\n            </div>\n          </div>\n\n          {/* 年利 */}\n          <div className=\"form-group\">\n            <div className=\"label-row\">\n              <label htmlFor=\"rate-input\">借入金利（年利）</label>\n              <span style={{ fontSize: '12px', color: 'var(--color-text-secondary)' }}>\n                {interestRate.toFixed(2)} %\n              </span>\n            </div>\n            <div data: {"agent":"specialist-kaito","file":"src/components/LoanTable.tsx","content":"import { useState, useMemo } from 'react';\nimport { YearlySchedule } from '../types';\n\ninterface LoanTableProps {\n  schedule?: YearlySchedule[];\n}\n\nexport default function LoanTable({ schedule = [] }: LoanTableProps) {\n  const [displayMode, setDisplayMode] = useState<'all' | 'five-years'>('all');\n\n  const filteredSchedule = useMemo(() => {\n    if (displayMode === 'all') {\n      return schedule;\n    }\n    const total = schedule.length;\n    return schedule.filter((item) => {\n      // 1年目、5の倍数年、最終年（重複排除）\n      return item.year === 1 || item.year % 5 === 0 || item.year === total;\n    });\n  }, [schedule, displayMode]);\n\n  const handleDownloadCsv = () => {\n    if (schedule.length === 0) return;\n\n    const headers = [\n      '返済年',\n      '年間元金返済(円)',\n      '年間利息支払(円)',\n      '年間返済合計(円)',\n      '年末ローン残高(円)',\n      '累計返済総額(円)',\n    ];\n\n    const rows = schedule.map((item) => [\n      `${item.year}年目`,\n      item.yearlyPrincipal,\n      item.yearlyInterest,\n      item.yearlyPrincipal + item.yearlyInterest,\n      item.remainingBalance,\n      item.totalPaid,\n    ]);\n\n    const csvContent =\n      '\\uFEFF' +\n      [headers.join(','), ...rows.map((row) => row.join(','))].join('\\r\\n');\n\n    const blob = new Blob([csvContent], { type: 年利（%。推奨範囲: 0.01%〜15.0%） */\n  interestRate: number;\n  /** 返済年数（年。推奨範囲: 1年〜50年） */\n  years: number;\n  /** 返済方式（'equal-payment': 元利均等返済, 'equal-principal': 元金均等返済） */\n  method: 'equal-payment' | 'equal-principal';\n  /** ボーナス時加算額（円 / 1回あたり・年2回。借入総額の50%以内推奨） */\n  bonusAmount: number;\n}\n\n/**\n * 年別返済シミュレーションスケジュール行\n */\nexport interface YearlySchedule {\n  /** 返済経過年（1〜返済年数） */\n  year: number;\n}\n\n/**\n * 住宅ローン計算結果\n */\nexport interface LoanCalculationResult {\n  /** 毎月の返済目安額（円。元金均等返済時は初月返済額） */\n  monthlyPayment: number;\n  /** 元金均等返済時の初月返済額（最大値・円） */\n  monthlyPaymentMax?: params;\n\n  if (principal <= 0 || years <= 0) {\n    return {\n      monthlyPayment: 0,\n      monthlyPaymentMax: 0,\n      monthlyPaymentMin: 0,\n      totalPayment: 0,\n      totalInterest: 0,\n      yearlySchedule: [],\n    };\n  }\n\n  const totalMonths = years * 12;\n  const monthlyRate = interestRate > 0 ? interestRate / 100 / 12 : 0;\n\n  // ボーナス返済分の元金按分計算（ボーナス返済がある場合）\n  // bonusAmount は年2回（1回あたり）の加算返済額\n  const bonusCount = years * 2;\n  const semiAnnualRate = interestRate > 0 ? interestRate / 100 / 2 : 0;\n\n  let bonusPrincipal = 0;\n  if (bonusAmount > 0 && bonusCount > 0) {\n    if (semiAnnualRate > 0.000001) {\n      // 元利均等でのボーナス現在価値相当\n      const factor = Math.pow(1 + semiAnnualRate, bonusCount);\n      const denominator = semiAnnualRate * factor;\n      if (denominator > 0 && factor > 1) {\n        const calculatedBonusPrincipal = Math.round((bonusAmount * (factor - 1)) / denominator);\n        bonusPrincipal = Math.min(principal * 0.5, Math.max(0, calculatedBonusPrincipal));\n      } else {\n        bonusPrincipal = Math.min(principal * 0.5, Math.max(0, bonusAmount * bonusCount));\n      }\n    } else {\n      bonusPrincipal = Math.min(principal * 0.5, Math.max(0, bonusAmount * bonusCount));\n    }\n  }\n\n  const monthlyLoanPrincipal = Math.max(0, principal - bonusPrincipal);\n\n  let firstMonthPayment = 0;\n  let lastMonthPayment = 0;\n  let totalPaymentSum = 0;\n  let totalInterestSum = 0;\n\n  let currentBalance = principal;\n  const schedule: YearlySchedule[] = [];\n\n  if (method === 'equal-payment') {\n    // 【元利均等返済】\n    let standardMonthlyPayment = 0;\n    if (monthlyRate > 0) {\n      const factor = Math.pow(1 + monthlyRate, totalMonths);\n      standardMonthlyPayment =\n        factor > 1 ? (monthlyLoanPrincipal * monthlyRate * factor) / (factor - 1) : 0;\n    } else {\n      standardMonthlyPayment = totalMonths > 0 ? monthlyLoanPrincipal / totalMonths : 0;\n    }\n\n    firstMonthPayment = Math.round(standardMonthlyPayment);\n    lastMonthPayment = Math.round(standardMonthlyPayment);\n\n    let cumulativeTotalPaid = 0;\n\n    for (let y = 1; y <= years; y++) {\n      let yearPrincipal = 0;\n      let yearInterest = 0;\n\n      for (let m = 1; m <= 12; m++) {\n        if (currentBalance <= 0) break;\n\n        const interest = currentBalance * monthlyRate;\n        let pmt = standardMonthlyPayment;\n\n        // ボーナス月（6月・12月）加算\n        if ((m === 6 || m === 12) && bonusAmount > 0) {\n          pmt += bonusAmount;\n        }\n\n        let principalPaid = pmt - interest;\n\n        // 最終月の残債超過調整\n        if (principalPaid > currentBalance || (y === years && m === 12)) {\n          principalPaid = currentBalance;\n          pmt = principalPaid + interest;\n        }\n\n        currentBalance -= principalPaid;\n        yearPrincipal += principalPaid;\n        yearInterest += interest;\n        cumulativeTotalPaid += pmt;\n      }\n\n      totalPaymentSum += yearPrincipal + yearInterest;\n      totalInterestSum += yearInterest;\n\n      schedule.push({\n        year: y,\n        remainingBalance: Math.max(0, Math.round(currentBalance)),\n        yearlyPrincipal: Math.round(yearPrincipal),\n        yearlyInterest: Math.round(yearInterest),\n        totalPaid: Math.round(cumulativeTotalPaid),\n      });\n    }\n  } else {\n    // 【元金均等返済】\n    const monthlyPrincipalFixed = totalMonths > 0 ? monthlyLoanPrincipal / totalMonths : 0;\n    const bonusPrincipalFixed = bonusCount > 0 ? bonusPrincipal / bonusCount : 0;\n\n    firstMonthPayment = Math.round(monthlyPrincipalFixed + principal * monthlyRate);\n    lastMonthPayment = Math.max(\n      0,\n      Math.round(monthlyPrincipalFixed + monthlyPrincipalFixed * monthlyRate)\n    );\n\n    let cumulativeTotalPaid = 0;\n\n    for (let y = 1; y <= years; y++) {\n      let yearPrincipal = 0;\n      let yearInterest = 0;\n\n      for (let m = 1; m <= 12; m++) {\n        if (currentBalance <= 0) break;\n\n        const interest = currentBalance * monthlyRate;\n        let principalPaid = monthlyPrincipalFixed;\n\n        // ボーナス月（6月・12月）の元金加算\n        if ((m === 6 || m === 12) && bonusPrincipalFixed > 0) {\n          principalPaid += bonusPrincipalFixed;\n        }\n\n        // 最終月調整\n        if (principalPaid > currentBalance || (y === years && m === 12)) {\n          principalPaid = currentBalance;\n        }\n\n        const pmt = principalPaid + interest;\n        currentBalance -= principalPaid;\n        yearPrincipal += principalPaid;\n        yearInterest += interest;\n        cumulativeTotalPaid += pmt;\n      }\n\n      totalPaymentSum += yearPrincipal + yearInterest;\n      totalInterestSum += yearInterest;\n\n      schedule.push({\n        year: y,\n        remainingBalance: Math.max(0, Math.round(currentBalance)),\n        yearlyPrincipal: Math.round(yearPrincipal),\n        yearlyInterest: Math.round(yearInterest),\n        totalPaid: Math.round(cumulativeTotalPaid),\n      });\n    }\n  }\n\n  return {\n    monthlyPayment: firstMonthPayment,\n    monthlyPaymentMax: method === 'equal-principal' ? firstMonthPayment : undefined,\n    monthlyPaymentMin: method === 'equal-principal' ? lastMonthPayment : undefined,\n    totalPayment: Math.round(totalPaymentSum),\n    totalInterest: Math.round(totalInterestSum),\n    yearlySchedule: schedule,\n  };\n}","language":"typescript"},{"path":"src/components/LoanChart.tsx","content":"import { useState, useMemo } from 'react';\nimport {\n  ResponsiveContainer,\n  AreaChart,\n  Area,\n  BarChart,\n  Bar,\n  XAxis,\n  YAxis,\n  Tooltip,\n  Legend,\n  CartesianGrid,\n} from 'recharts';\nimport { YearlySchedule } from '../types';\n\ninterface LoanChartProps {\n  schedule?: YearlySchedule[];\n}\n\ntype ChartMode = 'balance' | 'cumulative' | 'breakdown';\n\nconst formatTenThousandYen = (val: number): string => {\n  const manYen = Math.round(val / 10000);\n  return `${manYen.toLocaleString()}万円`;\n};\n\nconst formatTooltipValue = (value: number | string | undefined): string => {\n  const numericVal = typeof value === 'number' ? './utils/loanCalculator';\nimport LoanChart from './components/LoanChart';\nimport LoanTable from './components/LoanTable';\n\n// 入力範囲定数の一元管理\nconst LIMITS = {\n  PRINCIPAL: { MIN: 100, MAX: 20000, STEP_SLIDER: 1 },\n} as const;\n\nexport default function App() {\n  // 借入額（万円単位で保持し、計算時に円に変換）\n  const [principalManYen, setPrincipalManYen] = useState<number>(3500);\n  // 年利（%）\n  const [interestRate, setInterestRate] = useState<number>(0.7);\n  // 返済期間（年）\n  const [years, setYears] = useState<number>(35);\n  // 返済方式\n  const [method, setMethod] = useState<'equal-payment' | 'equal-principal'>('equal-payment');\n  // ボーナス加算額（1回あたり・万円）\n  const [bonusManYen, setBonusManYen] = useState<number>(0);\n\n  // 計算パラメータ作成\n  const loanParams: LoanParams = useMemo(() => {\n    return {\n      principal: principalManYen * 10000,\n      interestRate: interestRate,\n      years: years,\n      method: method,\n      bonusAmount: bonusManYen * 10000,\n    };\n  }, [principalManYen, interestRate, years, method, bonusManYen]);\n\n  // ローン計算の実行\n  const result = useMemo(() => {\n    return calculateLoan(loanParams);\n  }, [loanParams]);\n\n  // 借入額入力ハンドラ\n  const handlePrincipalChange = (val: number) => {\n    const clamped = Math.max(\n      LIMITS.PRINCIPAL.MIN,\n      Math.min(LIMITS.PRINCIPAL.MAX, isNaN(val) ? LIMITS.PRINCIPAL.MIN : val)\n    );\n    setPrincipalManYen(clamped);\n  };\n\n  // 金利入力ハンドラ\n  const handleRateChange = (val: number) => {\n    const clamped = Math.max(\n      LIMITS.RATE.MIN,\n      Math.min(LIMITS.RATE.MAX, isNaN(val) ? LIMITS.YEARS.MIN : val)\n    );\n    setYears(clamped);\n  };\n\n  // ボーナス返済額変更ハンドラ\n  const handleBonusChange = (val: number) => {\n    const clamped = Math.max(\n      LIMITS.BONUS.MIN,\n      Math.min(LIMITS.BONUS.MAX, isNaN(val) ? LIMITS.BONUS.MIN : val)\n    );\n    setBonusManYen(clamped);\n  };\n\n  return (\n    <div className=\"app-container\">\n      <header className=\"app-header\">\n        <span className=\"header-badge\">Mortgage Calculator</span>\n        <h1>住宅ローン返済シミュレーター</h1>\n        <p>\n          借入額・金利・返済年数から毎月の返済額・返済総額・利息内訳を即座に計算し、年別推移をグラフと表で確認できます。\n        </p>\n      </header>\n\n      <div className=\"calculator-grid\">\n        {/* 入力フォームカード */}\n        <section className=\"card\">\n          <h2 className=\"card-title\">ローン借入条件の設定</h2>\n\n          {/* 借入金額 */}\n          <div className=\"form-group\">\n            <div className=\"label-row\">\n              <label htmlFor=\"principal-input\">借入希望額</label>\n              <span style={{ fontSize: '12px', color: 'var(--color-text-secondary)' }}>\n                {principalManYen >= 10000\n                  ? `${Math.floor(principalManYen / 10000)}億${\n                      principalManYen % 10000 > 0 ? className=\"number-input\"\n                  min={LIMITS.PRINCIPAL.MIN}\n                  max={LIMITS.PRINCIPAL.MAX}\n                  step={LIMITS.PRINCIPAL.STEP_NUMBER}\n                  value={principalManYen}\n                  onChange={(e) => handlePrincipalChange(Number(e.target.value))}\n                />\n                <span>万円</span>\n              </div>\n              <input\n                type=\"range\"\n                className=\"slider\"\n                min={LIMITS.PRINCIPAL.MIN}\n                max={LIMITS.PRINCIPAL.MAX}\n                step={LIMITS.PRINCIPAL.STEP_SLIDER}\n                value={principalManYen}\n                onChange={(e) => handlePrincipalChange(Number(e.target.value))}\n              />\n            </div>\n
